Rook at g4. King is at g2.
If King moves to h3:
Rook at g4 does not attack h3.
Rook at a1 does not attack h3.
Check: a1 to h1 is possible, but h3 is not on h1.
Is any other black piece attacking h3?
Black pawn at h6? Pawns capture diagonally. A pawn at h6 attacks g5 and i5 (non-existent). So h6 does not attack h3.
Is the King at h3 safe? Yes.

If King moves to f3:
Rook at a1 can move to f1 and check the King.
Rook at g4 does not attack f3.

If King moves to f2:
Rook at a1 can move to f1 and check the King.

So g2h3 seems like the most restrictive move for the opponent's immediate checks.
